# zk.de forum: staff sweep

Ticket: https://github.com/theaustinschool/game-design-map/issues/7
Scope: every public forum.zettelkasten.de discussion and comment by the site's blog authors, Sascha Fast (forum `Sascha`, userID 4) and Christian Tietze (forum `ctietze`, userID 3), 2017-05-30 to 2026-11-04.
Swept: 2026-11-04

## Coverage log

Method: `GET https://forum.zettelkasten.de/api/v2/discussions?insertUserID=<id>&limit=100&page=<n>` and `GET .../api/v2/comments?insertUserID=<id>&limit=100&page=<n>`, paged until no `rel="next"` link, 1.5 s between requests, anonymous. The API returns each post's full HTML body; quoted text from other users (`<blockquote>`) was set aside so only the staff member's own words were read. ## Known gaps

- **Posts hidden from anonymous readers.** The API filters rows after paging: page 1 of `comments?insertUserID=4&limit=100` returned 93 rows, not 100. This is consistent with posts in categories closed to anonymous users (members-only or staff areas). That cause is inferred, not confirmed. Their number and content cannot be checked without a forum account. All counts here cover only the anonymously visible posts.
- **Profile activity pages need sign-in.** `https://forum.zettelkasten.de/profile/<name>` redirects to `/entry/signin`, so the per-profile post counts the forum keeps could not be compared against the API totals.
- **Thread context.** Only the staff members' own words were read. Other users' posts, and text the staff quoted from others, were not read. A claim's scope (for example, a reply that answers one user's specific setup) may be narrower than the claim text suggests; the permalink gives the full context.
- **Edited posts.** The API returns the current body. Earlier versions of edited posts are not visible.
- **Images and attachments.** Screenshots and attached images of example zettels were not read; only the post text was.
- **Other staff.** Only the two blog authors listed at https://zettelkasten.de/authors/ count as staff here. Forum moderators who are not blog authors, and guest blog authors, are out of scope.
